UEFA European Cup Matches 2012/2013
> 2014
This page lists the matches of European club football in the season 2012/2013. These match results are input for the calculation of coefficients and rankings (menu at top of the page). See the database for an overview of all matches of European Cup football, or search in all matches since 1955.
